# The unit tests, run in <repo>, a worktree of branch vmaf/phase-3, at the commit below with a clean working tree (docs/DECISIONS.md P-028, P-032, P-060, P-065, P-066, P-073, P-074, P-077, P-078, P-087): each command, then its output and exit status. The Python suites run with TMPDIR in a scratch directory outside the repository, <scratch>. `uptime`, first and last, gives the machine's load averages, what else was running taking its share of them, and `sw_vers` the macOS version.
